### Phase 20, Chat surface polish, markdown both ways + a legible instrument strip (109)

- [x] **T109**, **Eight pieces of chat-surface feedback: markdown in your own messages, a two-row instrument strip, live draft accounting, and right-clickable images.**
  - **(1) Hover bleed in Command Center.** `PanelList` is a `.card` (16px radius) with `divide-y` rows, but the rows' `hover:bg-surface-2` fill painted square into the rounded top/bottom corners. Added `overflow-hidden` to the list so rows are clipped to the card's radius. Verified by hit-testing rather than by eye: `elementFromPoint` two pixels inside the card's top-left bounding-box corner now lands on the enclosing `<section>` (clipped away) while the straight mid-edge still lands on the row `<button>`.
  - **(2) Markdown on both sides.** User messages rendered as `whitespace-pre-wrap` text, so a typed dash list stayed dashes. They now go through `Markdown` too. The renderer was also rebuilt from "split on blank lines, classify the whole block" to a **line scanner**, because the old version only made a list when *every* line of a block was a bullet, exactly the wrong rule for how people type ("nekko-agent project." followed by dashes with no blank line). It now handles bullet/numbered runs **nested by indentation**, a bullet↔number switch at the same level starting a new list, headings, block quotes, rules, pipe tables, fenced code, and inline bold / *italic* / ~~strike~~ / code / links / bare-URL autolinking, with single newlines kept as `<br>`. Italics are stars-only and both delimiters must hug their text, so `some_long_name` and `2 * 3 * 4` don't become emphasis. New `Markdown.test.tsx` (11 cases) pins all of it, including fenced code not being parsed as markdown.
  - **(3) Context that moves as you type.** The composer gauge and the Context Inspector both only counted *sent* messages. `ContextGauge` gained a `draftTokens` prop, and the pane mirrors its draft into a new `store.draftBySession` (undebounced, so the right-hand panel and the gauge move in the same frame) which the Inspector reads. Both surfaces show the draft as a renderer-only **"Your message"** provenance source (accent-colored, in `SOURCE_META`), and the panel's caption switched from "updates every reply" to "updates as you type" / "includes what you're typing".
  - **(4) Attachments inside the composer.** Pending image thumbnails were `absolute bottom-full` above the composer, i.e. floating over the instrument strip. They now render as the first child *inside* the `.composer` card, a horizontally scrolling thumbnail strip with a `border-b` hairline above the textarea, thumbnails 64px with the existing hover ✕.
  - **(5+6+7) The instrument strip: two rows, three shapes.** Effort wrapped because its wrapper `div` could shrink (only the inner button had `shrink-0`), and a single row of seven identical `.chip` pills gave menus, toggles, and actions the same weight. The strip is now **row one, how this agent runs** (Mode, Tools left; a hairline divider then Offline, Incognito pushed right) and **row two, which brain it uses** (model, Thinking, Effort left; Automate right). Three new control families in `styles.css` replace `.chip` here: `.ctl-menu` (bordered transparent field, muted label, caret) for value pickers, `.ctl-toggle` (no background until hover; state carried by color plus a `.ctl-dot` that lights up) for switches, and plain icon buttons for actions. Offline lights accent, Incognito uses `.ctl-toggle-neutral` (plain ink) so the strip has exactly one accent state, and the passive "streamed reasoning" indicator uses `.ctl-toggle-on` since a `<span>` can't carry `aria-pressed`.
  - **(8) Right-click a chat image.** New `ImageMenu`, portalled to `body` at the pointer (flipped when it would run off an edge), with **Copy image** and **Save image…**, wired to composer thumbnails, sent-message thumbnails, and the full-size lightbox. Two traps found in verification, both fixed: the renderer's CSP has no `data:` in `connect-src`, so `fetch(dataURL)` fails with "Failed to fetch" (the data URL is now decoded by hand into a Blob, and the PNG re-encode draws straight from the source URL, no object URL); and closing the menu on any document `mousedown` unmounted the item before its click could fire, since a portalled menu's press reaches the document listener anyway (now tested for containment against the menu element). Escape is captured on `window`, one step ahead of the lightbox's document-capture handler, so the first Escape closes the menu and the second closes the preview.
  - **Verified** over an isolated web edition (own port 1499 + throwaway data dir, seeded with a markdown-heavy transcript): the user bubble renders `<p>` + nested `<ul>`(disc)/`<ul>`(circle) + `<br>`, the assistant bubble exercises em/strong/s/code/a/ol/ul/blockquote/hr/table/pre; typing 142 characters moved the gauge 424 → 460 tok and added "Your message 36 tok" to the panel's breakdown; the attachment strip measures 517-602 with the textarea starting at 602 and the control rows ending at 478 (no overlap); the strip holds two rows with no wrap and no overflow at 1280, 900, and 640 wide; probes of each control state confirm transparent backgrounds at rest with accent (#6d5efc) for Offline-on, plain ink for Incognito-on, and lit dots; the image menu opens over the lightbox (z 60 vs 50), reaches `navigator.clipboard.write` with a 77-byte `image/png` ClipboardItem (the write itself only fails on `Document is not focused`, since the verification pane isn't focused; permission is `granted`), and Save produces a `blob:` href named `nekko-agent-image.png`. Full typecheck x8 + 130 core / **18** desktop / 33 cloud tests + desktop/web build green. · Done: 2026-07-27

### Phase 21, The chat's brain sticks, the model menu stays in the pane, the Spec panel follows the folders (110)

- [x] **T110**, **Three workbench bugs: a chat that couldn't send after a tab switch, a model menu hanging outside its pane, and a Spec panel showing the wrong project.**
  - **(1) The reported bug, sending did nothing after switching tabs.** The model picker's choice lived only in `ChatPane`'s local state (`onModel` persisted `autoModel` and nothing else). The workbench mounts only the pane you're looking at, so a tab switch tore the pane down and the rebuilt one re-derived provider/model from `session.providerId`/`modelId`, which are only written when a reply is actually sent. Pick LM Studio in a fresh chat, switch tabs, and you came back on the chat's old provider, and if that provider exposes no models (a local server that isn't running) `modelId` resolved to `null`, the chip read **"No model"**, and `send()` hit a bare `if (!useModel) return;`: a lit send button that did nothing, no toast, no log. Two fixes. `onModel` now parks the pick on the chat itself (`setSessionOptions` gained `providerId` + `modelId` through the whole chain: `shared/ipc.ts`, `host.ts`, `sessions.ts`), so provider and model survive a tab switch, a closed pane, and a restart. And the silent guards are gone: a new `requireBrain(text)` returns `{ providerId, modelId }` or explains what's missing, "Add a model provider in Models first" with no providers, "still loading its model" during the async window right after a mount, and "No models available from `<provider>`. Start it, or pick another model below the chat." when the provider lists none. Used by `send`, `/goal`, and edit-and-resend (which had the same silent return), and it keeps the draft and attachments intact so nothing is lost to a failed send.
  - **(2) The model menu hung outside the pane.** The picker's panel was `absolute bottom-8 right-0` at `w-80` (320px) under a `max-w-[240px]` chip that sits at the **left** edge of its row, so right-anchoring pushed ~80px of menu past the pane's left edge, over the sidebar. It now opens `bottom-full left-0` (the file's own convention for the `@`/`/`/`+` menus, and no magic 32px offset) with `max-w-[calc(100vw-2rem)]`, so it grows rightwards into the pane it belongs to.
  - **(3) The Spec panel didn't follow the folder you picked.** `SpecPanel` reset its workspace selection only when the *set* of the chat's folders changed, keeping the current pick whenever it was still a member. Making a different folder primary in the Folders section directly above (the pane's own affordance for switching project) left the panel on the old project: it read "Primary · nekko-agent" while the row above said mynichi was primary, and Build/Update wrote to the wrong repo. It now snaps to `session.workspaceId` whenever the primary (or the folder set) changes; its own select still overrides, until the choice above moves again.
  - **Verified** over the web edition against a throwaway scratch chat in an isolated data dir, then cleaned up (chat deleted, temp provider removed): the Spec panel showed `…\nekko-agent\spec.md` + `tasks.md`, clicking the **mynichi** folder row flipped the badges *and* moved the panel to `…\mynichi\spec.md` + `tasks.md` in the same beat, and picking "Supporting · nekko-agent" from the select overrode it back. With the chat pinned to a provider exposing no models, the chip read "No model · Scratch (dead)" and clicking send produced **"No models available from Scratch (dead). Start it, or pick another model below the chat."** with the typed draft still in the box (previously: nothing at all). Picking `google/gemma-4-12b` then opening a second tab and switching back kept the chip on that model, and `session:get` confirmed `providerId: lmstudio-x` / `modelId: google/gemma-4-12b` on disk. Menu geometry measured open: card `left 416 → right 736` inside a pane whose content starts at 416 (previously `left 297`, i.e. 119px into the sidebar), bottom 8px clear of the chip. Full typecheck x8 + 18 desktop tests + web build green, no console errors. · Done: 2026-07-28

### Phase 22, Auto profiles + a real markdown/editing pane + the folders-first right panel (111)

- [x] **T111**, **Eight pieces of chat + models feedback: Auto that says what it will do, markdown that reads like a document, a file pane you can actually edit in, and a folders-first right panel.**
  - **(1) A chat with no model asks for one.** The store's `selectProvider` auto-picked `models[0]` whenever the current model wasn't in a provider's list, so "no model selected" never happened, and the guess was often wrong: LM Studio serves speech and embedding models from the same endpoint, and its first entry here is `whisper-large-v3`. Verification caught exactly that (a fresh chat sitting on Whisper). It now leaves `activeModelId` unset instead, and `ChatPane` stops falling back to `m[0]` too, so a chat without a model **says so** in three places: the empty state ("Pick a model to get started" + a button that opens the picker), a nudge above the composer, and the model chip itself reading "Choose a model" in accent. `requireBrain` also opens the picker rather than only toasting. The picker's open state moved up into the pane so all three affordances drive the same menu.
  - **(2) Auto profiles + a visible pick.** `pickAutoModel` replaces `recommendModel` as the primary API (the old function stays as a thin id-only wrapper) and returns the **reasoning** with the choice: `{ modelId, name, tier, complex, reason }`. Three per-chat profiles (`session.autoQuality`, new through the `setSessionOptions` chain): **Cheap** always takes the smallest capable model, **Quality** always the strongest, **Normal** reads the prompt (the previous behavior). Cheap and Quality deliberately ignore the prompt, a spend instruction that silently changes its mind is worse than one that doesn't. New `isChatModel` filters candidates so Auto never routes a reply to Whisper, an embedding model, a reranker, OCR, or an image model (falling back to the raw list only if nothing survives). The strip gained an **Auto** profile chip beside the model chip, shown only while Auto is selected, and the "→ &lt;model&gt;" preview now renders with an empty draft too, titled with the reason. 6 new shared tests (14 in `model-select.test.ts`).
  - **(3) Markdown that reads like a document.** `.md` files did render through `Markdown`, but that renderer is tuned for chat bubbles: headings were `<div class="font-semibold">` barely louder than body text, every hard-wrapped line became a `<br>`, and embedded HTML (`<div align="center">`, `<table>`, `<img>`) was dumped as literal text, which is why a README read as a wall of plain text. New opt-in **`doc` mode**: real `h1`–`h6` with a size scale, reflowed paragraphs, roomier lists/tables/quotes, task-list checkboxes, HTML comments hidden, block-level HTML reduced to its text (erased, never mounted, markdown from anywhere is untrusted), and `<img>` folded into the same handling as `![]()`. Images and relative links become chips/buttons that **open the real file** via a new `basePath` prop, because the renderer's CSP allows only same-origin and `data:` images so a remote or on-disk one can't be shown inline. The link matcher accepts any path-or-anchor target but still refuses to read `rows[0](x)` as a link. Chat mode is byte-identical; 8 new cases (19 in `Markdown.test.tsx`).
  - **(4) The file pane edits like an editor.** Save was manual-only and a controlled textarea has no working native undo. Added **auto-save** (debounced on an idle pause, remembered in `localStorage`, flushed on unmount and `beforeunload`, off falls back to the Save button), an **own undo/redo history** (Ctrl+Z / Ctrl+Shift+Z / Ctrl+Y, snapshots carry the selection, a typing run inside 600ms coalesces into one step, capped at 200), **copy / cut / paste** toolbar actions (copy falls back to the whole file with no selection), Tab-to-indent, save-on-blur, and a save-state readout (Saving shortly → Saved) replacing the bare dirty dot.
  - **(5+6) Folders first, then context, with a resizable split.** The right panel's Folders section was a list of buttons and the file tree lived in a separate dockable **Files** side-pane, one pane over from the folder list it duplicated. The tree moved **into** the Folders section: each folder is an accordion whose row still carries primary/supporting/exclude/remove and whose body is its lazy `DirTree`. Below it, the **Context** section, split by a draggable divider, VS Code style: with every tree collapsed Folders is content-height and Context owns the panel; opening one gives Folders the dragged height, capped so Context keeps a usable minimum. The asked-for height is kept unclamped and only capped for display, so freeing space returns it rather than stranding the split where a cramped panel left it. `FilesSidePane` and the store's whole `filesPane*` slice are gone.
  - **(7) The panel belongs to the workbench, not to a chat pane.** Found during verification: with `ContextInspector` rendered inside `ChatPane`, clicking a file in the new explorer opened the file pane and **took the explorer with it**, since the chat pane was no longer active. It now renders in `WorkbenchView` beside `<main>`, following `activeSessionId` (it already had an empty state for "no chat"), and the workbench sidebar's panel button, freed up by the Files pane's removal, toggles it. The **Resources** dock is collapsible for the same space reason, keeping CPU/VRAM in its header when closed, and it publishes its rect either way so the panel re-measures the split when it opens or closes.
  - **(8) Models page.** **Stop server** now renders only for a local provider whose connection actually tested `ok` (it was always rendered and merely disabled when offline, a greyed button inviting a click that can't work). The **load** chip gained a real hover state (new `.chip-action`: accent tint + inset ring), and a **loaded** chip now reads "loaded" at rest and "unload" under the cursor with a danger fill, so one chip carries the state and the action.
  - **Verified** over the web edition against this machine's real LM Studio + Anthropic providers: all three Auto profiles resolve live and differently (Cheap/Normal `qwen/qwen3.6-27b`, Quality `nvidia/nemotron-3-nano-omni`, Normal on "Refactor and debug the distributed lock" moving up to Nemotron) after the non-chat filter took Whisper and `unlimited-ocr` out of contention; clearing the default model (then restoring it) produced all three "choose a model" affordances and the CTA opened the picker; `README.md` rendered `H1 🐾 Nekko Agent / H2 Overview / H2 Download / H3 macOS…` with four image chips and **zero** leftover HTML; a scratch file typed into showed "Saving shortly" → "Saved" and the text **on disk**, with undo restoring the previous content and redo putting it back; the split measured 143/176 with the dock open, 260/379 with it collapsed, and restored on reopen; opening a file from the explorer kept the panel mounted. One harness limitation worth recording: the preview pane runs `document.hidden === true`, so it fires **no** `requestAnimationFrame` and **no** `ResizeObserver` callbacks, hence the dock's rect doubling as an explicit dependency of the measuring effect. Full typecheck x8 + 136 core / 26 desktop tests + web build green, no console errors. · Done: 2026-07-28

### Phase 29, Marketing site: stop pinning stale CSS and JS (120)

- [x] **T120**, **The site's own CSS and JS were cached `immutable` for a year, so shipped changes never reached returning visitors.** `vercel.json` sent `Cache-Control: public, max-age=31536000, immutable` for `css|js|png|jpg|jpeg|svg|webp|ico|woff2`. That header is correct for content-hashed bundles (hypergate's site is a Vite build, so its `index-DXJ-KgGb.js` genuinely never changes), but this site is hand-written: `styles.css` and `site.js` keep their names forever. Caught while verifying T119 on the live site: the deployed `styles.css` contained the new tokens (`curl` confirmed) while the browser rendered the **old** button styling from a `transferSize: 0` disk-cache hit, i.e. new `index.html` against a year-old stylesheet, a mix that can look arbitrarily broken.
  - **Split the header rule**: images and fonts keep `immutable`; `css|js` now get `public, max-age=0, must-revalidate`, so each load revalidates against the ETag and a 304 costs nothing on a 15 KB file.
  - **One-time cache-buster**: a header change can't release copies already pinned, since a pinned response is never revalidated. `index.html` (which was never `immutable`) now references `styles.css?v=2` and `site.js?v=2`, which is a new URL to every cache. With `must-revalidate` in place, future edits need no further bump.
  - **Verified**: `vercel.json` parses; both assets return the new header on the deployed site and revalidate rather than replay from disk; a browser holding the pinned pre-fix copies renders T118 + T119 correctly after the `?v=2` reference lands. · [spec](SPEC.md#distribution--platforms) · Done: 2026-08-03



### Phase 27, Marketing site: the hero downloads, the menu behaves, the shell gets a one-liner (118)

- [x] **T118**, **The hero button downloads, the dropdown stops colliding with the page, and terminal people get a copyable one-liner.** Four changes to `apps/website`, all in the download path.
  - **The hero CTA is the real download button.** It was an anchor to `#download`, so the first click only scrolled you to a second button. The smart picker markup (primary + caret + menu) is now duplicated in the hero and driven by the same code, so a hero click starts the actual `.exe`/`.dmg`/`.AppImage` download for the detected machine, subtitle and version included. `site.js`'s single-instance globals (`dlPick`/`dlMain`/`dlMainTitle`/…) became `setupPick(root)` over `[data-dl-pick]`, one closure per instance, with `closeOtherMenus()` so opening one closes the other. Ids stay only where ARIA needs them (`aria-controls`, `aria-labelledby`); everything else moved to data attributes. Without JS both instances are still plain links to `/releases/latest`.
  - **"Star on GitHub" became a quiet outline pill.** New `.btn-ghost`: fully rounded, hairline border, near-transparent fill, trailing `↗`, reading "View GitHub repo". `.cta-row` got `align-items: stretch` so the pill matches the split button's height instead of floating short beside it.
  - **The dropdown was painting under the footer.** `main`/`header`/`footer` all shared `z-index: 1`, and `main` creates a stacking context, so the menu (`z-index: 20` inside `.dl-pick`) could never rise above the later-in-DOM footer: footer text ran straight across the menu items. `main` is now `z-index: 2`, `.dl-pick` trades `isolation: isolate` for `z-index: 30` (isolation trapped the menu below any transformed sibling that followed it), the panel background went near-opaque (`rgba(3,11,24,0.97)`) with a stronger shadow, and the menu **flips above the button** when the room below is smaller than the menu and worse than the room above, capping `max-height` to whatever side it lands on and re-placing on scroll/resize. Also: `min-width: 100%` so the panel is never narrower than the button it hangs off, no dead top padding on the first group label, and the "yours" marker on your build is a cyan outline chip instead of `' ·  yours'` glued onto the hint text. The pop-in animates **transform only**: it used to fade from `opacity: 0`, which leaves the panel invisible wherever animations never advance (hidden tabs, embedded webviews, the same throttling the scroll reveals already work around).
  - **One-liner for people who'd rather stay in a shell.** Under the button, a mono command box with a `❯` prompt and a Copy button on its right (stacked underneath at ≤560px): PowerShell `iwr <asset> -OutFile nekko-agent-setup.exe; .\nekko-agent-setup.exe` on Windows, `curl -L <asset> -o Nekko Agent.dmg && open Nekko Agent.dmg` on macOS, `curl -L … && chmod +x … && ./Nekko Agent.AppImage` on Linux. It uses the exact asset URL the button resolved, so it is hidden until the Releases API answers rather than shipping a guessed URL, and hidden entirely for phones and unknown platforms. The command wraps instead of scrolling (nobody should paste what they can't read), and Copy uses `navigator.clipboard` with an `execCommand` fallback and a text-selection last resort, flipping to a green "Copied" for 1.9s.
  - **Verified** in real Chrome against the static server: two pickers both resolving to the live v0.1.5 `-x64.exe` asset URL; menu opaque and flipping up in both hero and download section with no footer bleed; a real click on Copy putting the full 138-character command on the clipboard and the button going to "Copied"; the command's download half run for real in PowerShell (82.7 MB, `MZ` header, valid PE); 390px-wide layout stacking the pill under the button and Copy under the command; no console errors. · [spec](SPEC.md#distribution--platforms) · Done: 2026-08-03

### Phase 27, Border beam (118)

- [x] **T118**, **Border-beam animation, in brand colors.** The border-beam pattern (a beam of light lapping an element's border, from Jakub Antalik's Border Beam UI demo on recent.design), recolored violet→cyan to match the theme instead of the reference's rainbow. Two homes: the **chat composer** gains `.composer-beam` while a turn is streaming (a conic-gradient ring rotating via a registered `--beam-angle` custom property, masked to a 1.5px border with the content-box XOR trick, plus a faint `beam-wash` gradient drifting across the surface, background-position so it needs no registration); and the **marketing site**, where the hero window mock gets a slow quiet lap and the download pill a brighter one that speeds up on hover and hides while its menu is open. Both degrade to a static gradient ring without `@property` support and freeze under `prefers-reduced-motion`. Evidence in `docs/screenshots/border-beam-*`. · Done: 2026-08-05



### Phase 25, Marketing site: the harness pitch + smart download (115)

- [x] **T115**, **The site leads with local models, then argues the harness, and the download button knows your machine.** Three changes to `apps/website`, plus the GitHub Releases cleanup the rebrand missed.
  - **Chapter order.** "Local models, finally first-class" was chapter 02, behind a generic feature tour. It is now **01**, the first thing after the hero and window mock, and the nav leads with it.
  - **The harness chapter (02).** The old "Everything in one calm window" card grid (8 boxes, one of them the mascot) became **"The harness is the other half of the model"**: the argument that frontier labs ship raw capability and everything else you feel as "this model is good in my codebase" is scaffolding. Rebuilt as a **spec-sheet manifest instead of cards**, three groups (`.mf-group`) with a sticky label column and hairline-separated rows, auto-numbered by a CSS counter, accent bar on hover: **what it sees** (Context Inspector, grounded retrieval, guidelines + memory, living spec, prompt analyzer), **what it can reach** (sub-agents and swarms, skills, MCP both directions, connectors, terminating goal loops), **when it's wrong** (guardrails, sandbox, diff review, recovery). Every row is a shipped capability from `SPEC.md`, not aspiration. The mascot line moved to a one-line footnote and the JSON-LD `featureList` + `llms.txt` were rewritten to match, so answer engines get the same pitch.
  - **Smart download.** The three static platform cards (all three pointing at `/releases/latest`) are replaced by a **primary button that resolves the visitor's actual build** plus a caret menu of everything else. OS from `userAgentData.platform` with a UA fallback; Mac architecture from Chromium high-entropy hints, falling back to the WebGL renderer string, defaulting to Apple Silicon; iPadOS caught via `maxTouchPoints` (it claims to be a Mac), and phones get "install on your computer, then pair this one" instead of a useless installer link. `BUILDS` declares each published artifact with the regex that finds it, and one call to the **GitHub Releases API** turns those into direct asset URLs plus the version, shown in the button subtitle. Builds with no matching asset drop out of the menu on their own (v0.1.5 ships no Intel `.dmg`, so it isn't offered), and an offline or rate-limited fetch leaves every link pointing at the releases page. Menu is `role="menu"`, arrow-key navigable with wraparound, closes on Escape (focus back to the caret) or an outside click, and marks the build we picked for you.
  - **GitHub Releases.** Every published release was still titled **"Nekko Agent v0.1.x"** with Nekko Agent prose in the notes. Retitled v0.1.0 through v0.1.5 to **Nekko Agent v0.1.x** and rewrote each body, keeping the `xattr -cr "/Applications/Nekko Agent.app"` instruction accurate for those builds and adding a one-line historical note about the earlier name. Asset filenames are left alone on purpose: `latest.yml` references them, so renaming would break electron-updater for installed clients. · [spec](SPEC.md#distribution--platforms) · Done: 2026-08-03

### App-control permissions and isolated sessions (2026-10-03)

Technical approach: independent approval policy and session environment; default-deny capability broker at execution time, inherited without expansion by children. Container adapter discovery is read-only. Runtime provisioning is consent-gated. Isolated filesystem copies and conflict-aware apply require verification before enabling sandbox selection. Route or disable daemon-native execution until it honors the same policy. Do not expose a UI-only sandbox.

### T185: Agents wall new-chat steps, status glyphs, Spooky hat placement, delegation errors (2026-10-08) · [spec](SPEC.md#agents-wall-new-chat-steps-status-glyphs-and-composer-chrome-2026-10-08)

- [x] **T185** New chat in Add window is three steps with a one-click Start; wall windows show a rocket or Zz instead of a dot; the Spooky hat moved to the corner mascot only; the box under the wall composer and the second title row are gone; blank delegation route IDs get an actionable error.
  - **Error investigated.** The "Investigate Nekko usage and savings" chat (ChatGPT `gpt-6.1-sol`) called `spawn_agent` with `provider_id: ""` and `model_id: ""`. The host rejects blank routing IDs by design (`chat-routing.test.ts` holds that), and the message only said what was wrong. The next two attempts guessed `nekko-engine`/`gpt-sol` (model list unavailable, cause swallowed with no log) and `chatgpt-…`/`gpt-5.4` (not in that provider's catalog). Kept the rejection (rejected alternative: silently treating `""` as omitted, which would turn a malformed call into a different route without the model knowing); the error now says to omit the keys or call `delegation_targets`. The model-list catch now logs provider ID and error class, not the message, because provider errors can quote base URLs and keys.
  - **Picker.** Defaults: settings default model when its provider is enabled, else active provider + active model, else that provider's first model; folder default is `activeProjectId`, else the first folder. Folder picks go through `createSession(primary)` and `setSessionSupportingWorkspaces(rest)`. The model list's selected row is now styled inside the picker (the picker's generic button colour overrode the list's accent, so a pick looked unselected). Effort uses the existing global `EffortSlider`, not a per-chat effort; per-chat effort would need a session field and is left undone.
  - **Status.** `agentStatusOfLane` maps board lanes onto the sidebar `StatusIcon`, used by wall strips and the wall composer.
  - **Second title row.** Could not reproduce in a fresh fixture build: the frame's slot context was present. The user's running dev build showed it, consistent with a hot-reloaded `PaneFrame` module creating a new context object. `ChatHeader` now also takes `inWall` from `commandCenter`, so a wall chat never falls back to its own header regardless of context identity. Root cause is unconfirmed.
  - **Review fixes.** The picker no longer pairs the active provider with another provider's first catalog model or defaults to a disabled provider. An explicitly empty folder selection stays empty instead of falling back to the active project. A retained-session retry reapplies primary and supporting folders, including clearing old supporting folders; null host configuration responses are errors rather than silent success.
  - **Review verification.** 50 focused renderer tests and 81 host routing tests pass. `build:web` and full workspace typecheck pass after isolating dependency links to this worktree's built workspace packages. Initial failures were caused by parent-checkout workspace resolution and a missing worktree-local electron-vite link, not weakened checks. Wide/narrow (1400px/600px) light/dark fixture captures and picker interaction GIFs are being published to the shared pr-media release. A synthetic failed-options/failed-cleanup retry test passed after correcting the fixture workspace setter to return the Session required by its contract: one create only, primary folder cleared and empty supporting folders applied.
  - **Not done.** Image-capable inspection is unavailable in this review toolset, so captured images and motion are not claimed visually verified. Native window chrome and real providers/folders remain unexercised; original second-title cause remains unconfirmed. Inspect published matrix and motion before landing; CI on the final head must pass.

### T187: Playwright drives the in-app browser through Electron's own Chromium (2026-10-10) · [spec](SPEC.md#in-app-browser-automation-2026-10-10)

- [x] **T187** `playwright-core` (pinned 1.63.0, no install script, no browsers) added to the desktop app and kept external to the main bundle. Playwright connects with `connectOverCDP(transport)` over an in-process transport (`agentCdpTransport.ts`) that reaches each agent window through `webContents.debugger`: no remote-debugging port, only the chat's own windows as targets, a page-session command policy (no file uploads, no target discovery or attachment, no download or certificate overrides, HTTP(S) or about:blank navigation). Rejected alternative: `--remote-debugging-port` on loopback, which any local process (including the agent's own shell commands) could use to drive the Nekko UI. The `browser` tool keeps navigate/inspect/click/fill/close and adds wait, type, press, scroll, extract, evaluate, screenshot (pixels as chat images), tabs, tab_new, tab_switch, tab_close and logs in dedicated mode; existing (Stagehand) mode refuses the new ones. Validation is shared in `packages/shared/src/browser-tool.ts` and runs in both the host and the bridge. Debuggers are detached before a window is destroyed, since destroying an attached window stalled Electron's main thread in the smoke run. Done: 2026-10-10
  - Verified: unit tests (transport policy/routing, page actions, bridge tabs/screenshot/cleanup, host validation and approval text), `bundleExternals.test.ts`, typecheck, and `apps/desktop/scripts/browser-automation-smoke.mjs` under real Electron 44 (hidden windows, throwaway `--user-data-dir`, process tree killed, profile deleted). playwright-core also loaded from inside an asar archive and drove a hidden page.
  - [ ] Not verified: a full electron-builder installer build, macOS and Linux runs, and live model use of the new actions.
  - [ ] The `capture` tool's owned-window screenshot still uses `capturePage`, which returned a stale frame after scrolling a hidden window in the smoke run; the browser `screenshot` action uses Playwright instead. Move capture's owned-window path to a fresh frame too.

Decision: the bundled-catalog rule stays (our text, originals linked); third-party skills install verbatim from their own repository.
